00010000 <_start>:
    # 第二种方法, 因kernel拉起来后不会再返回, 所以此处可省略ebp压栈, 返回地址压栈, 直接通过esp+4后赋值给eax
    # mov 4(%esp), %eax   // 取出boot_info参数(esp后面有函数返回值, 因此esp需要+4), 并保存到eax寄存器中
    # push %eax           // 调用kernel_init前, 将eax(存放boot_info_t * 指针地址)作为入参保存到栈中

    # 第三种方法, 直接将esp+4(只需跳过函数返回值)指向的boot_info参数入栈, 因为拉起内核后不会再返回了, 无需保存ebp栈底指针
    push 4(%esp)
   10000:	ff 74 24 04          	push   0x4(%esp)

    # kernel_init(boot_info), 作为内核初始化函数入口, 该函数在 init.c 中实现
    // 调用void kernel_init(boot_info_t * boot_info)运行内核, 同时传入boot_info硬件参数信息
    call kernel_init  // call指令调用完成后, 还会返回
   10004:	e8 8b 7c 00 00       	call   17c94 <kernel_init>

    jmp $KERNEL_SELECTOR_CS, $gdt_reload

000102e3 <simple_switch>:
// esp 切换时无需保存, 只需要让esp保存在调用之前的状态
# simple_switch(uint32_t ** from, uint32_t * to)
.text
.global simple_switch
simple_switch:
    movl 4(%esp), %eax   // 取传入参数from->stack, 保存前一个任务的from->stack到 eax 寄存器
   102e3:	8b 44 24 04          	mov    0x4(%esp),%eax
    movl 8(%esp), %edx   // 取传入参数to->stack, 保存下一个任务的to->stack到 edx 寄存器
   102e7:	8b 54 24 08          	mov    0x8(%esp),%edx

    // 保存前一任务的状态寄存器- 保存到函数栈中, 等恢复时将之前栈中保存的寄存器弹出
    push %ebp
   102eb:	55                   	push   %ebp
    push %ebx
   102ec:	53                   	push   %ebx
    push %esi
   102ed:	56                   	push   %esi
    push %edi
   102ee:	57                   	push   %edi
    // eip寄存器已自动保存(即函数返回地址=eip)
    // esp 保存到task的stack中, 在task_t中添加 uint32_t * stack 用于保存esp

    // 实现栈的切换, 保存前一个任务的esp寄存器到from->stack栈中, 并加载下一个任务的to->stack到edx寄存器
    mov %esp, (%eax)     // from->stack = esp
   102ef:	89 20                	mov    %esp,(%eax)
    mov %edx, %esp       // esp = to->stack
   102f1:	89 d4                	mov    %edx,%esp

    // 加载下一任务的状态寄存器
    pop %edi
   102f3:	5f                   	pop    %edi
    pop %esi
   102f4:	5e                   	pop    %esi
    pop %ebx
   102f5:	5b                   	pop    %ebx
    pop %ebp
   102f6:	5d                   	pop    %ebp

    ret // 普通函数 ret指令即可返回
   102f7:	c3                   	ret

000102f8 <exception_handler_syscall>:
exception_handler_syscall:
    # 需保存系统调用上下文, 有以下三种类型
    # 1. 硬件会自动压栈的寄存器: ss, esp, arg0～4, cs, eip
    # 2. X86 pushal指令会自动压栈通用寄存器: eax, ecx, edx, ebx, esp, ebp, esi, edi
    // 保存前一任务的状态
    pusha
   102f8:	60                   	pusha
    # 3. 手动压栈的寄存器: ds, es, fs, gs
    push %ds
   102f9:	1e                   	push   %ds
    push %es
   102fa:	06                   	push   %es
    push %fs
   102fb:	0f a0                	push   %fs
    push %gs
   102fd:	0f a8                	push   %gs
    pushf
   102ff:	9c                   	pushf

    // 使用内核段寄存器, 避免使用应用层的. 此处是将eax保存的系统调用返回值给到应用层
    mov $(KERNEL_SELECTOR_DS), %eax
   10300:	b8 10 00 00 00       	mov    $0x10,%eax
    mov %eax, %ds
   10305:	8e d8                	mov    %eax,%ds
    mov %eax, %es
   10307:	8e c0                	mov    %eax,%es
    mov %eax, %fs
   10309:	8e e0                	mov    %eax,%fs
    mov %eax, %gs
   1030b:	8e e8                	mov    %eax,%gs

    // 调用处理函数
    // <<<Function Call Stack>>>
    mov %esp, %eax
   1030d:	89 e0                	mov    %esp,%eax
    push %eax
   1030f:	50                   	push   %eax
    # 4. 返回地址, call指令自动压入
    call do_handler_syscall
   10310:	e8 fe 0b 00 00       	call   10f13 <do_handler_syscall>
    add $4, %esp
   10315:	83 c4 04             	add    $0x4,%esp

    # 恢复中断上下文, 保存时逆序即可
    # 1. 手动出栈的寄存器
    // 再切换回来
    popf
   10318:	9d                   	popf
    pop %gs
   10319:	0f a9                	pop    %gs
    pop %fs
   1031b:	0f a1                	pop    %fs
    pop %es
   1031d:	07                   	pop    %es
    pop %ds
   1031e:	1f                   	pop    %ds
    # 2. popal指令出栈通用寄存器: eax, ecx, edx, ebx, esp, ebp, esi, edi
    popa
   1031f:	61                   	popa

    // 硬件会自动压栈的寄存器: ss, esp, arg0~4, cs, eip
    // 系统调用传递的5个参数也被硬件自动压入栈中, 此时栈中顺序为: ... ss, esp, arg0~4, cs, eip ...
    // 因此出栈时也需要跳过5个参数, 总计5*4=20字节, 不加会导致返回时ss, esp等寄存器不能正确取出, 返回到用户态时出现问题
    retf $(5*4)    // CS发生了改变, 需要使用远跳转才能从内核态返回用户态, far return 即返回到不同的代码段中
   10320:	ca 14 00             	lret   $0x14

00010323 <down2>:
 * size = 0x1010, bound = 0x1000
 * ~(bound-1) = ~0x0FFF = 0xFFFF1000
 * size & ~(bound-1) = 0x1010 & 0xFFFF1000 = 0x1000
 */
static inline uint32_t down2(uint32_t size, uint32_t bound)
{
   10323:	55                   	push   %ebp
   10324:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
    return size & ~(bound - 1);
   10326:	8b 45 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%eax
   10329:	f7 d8                	neg    %eax
   1032b:	23 45 08             	and    0x8(%ebp),%eax
}
   1032e:	5d                   	pop    %ebp
   1032f:	c3                   	ret

00010330 <up2>:
 * 向上对齐到页边界
 * size = 0x1010, bound = 0x1000
 * result = 0x2000
 */
static inline uint32_t up2(uint32_t size, uint32_t bound)
{
   10330:	55                   	push   %ebp
   10331:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
    return (size + bound - 1) & ~(bound - 1);
   10333:	8b 55 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%edx
   10336:	8b 45 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%eax
   10339:	01 d0                	add    %edx,%eax
   1033b:	8d 50 ff             	lea    -0x1(%eax),%edx
   1033e:	8b 45 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%eax
   10341:	f7 d8                	neg    %eax
   10343:	21 d0                	and    %edx,%eax
}
   10345:	5d                   	pop    %ebp
   10346:	c3                   	ret

00010347 <write_cr3>:
    return cr2;
}

// CR3寄存器保存页目录基地址(用于分页机制)
static inline void write_cr3(uint32_t v)
{
   10347:	55                   	push   %ebp
   10348:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
    __asm__ __volatile__("mov %[v], %%cr3"::[v]"r"(v));
   1034a:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   1034d:	0f 22 d8             	mov    %eax,%cr3
}
   10350:	90                   	nop
   10351:	5d                   	pop    %ebp
   10352:	c3                   	ret

000103ef <addr_alloc_init>:
 * uint32_t page_size  每一页物理内存的大小
 * 函数内不检查start和size的页边界, 由上层调用者检查
 */
static void addr_alloc_init(addr_alloc_t * alloc, uint8_t * bits,
                    uint32_t start, uint32_t size, uint32_t page_size)
{

   103f0:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp

   10407:	8b 55 10             	mov    0x10(%ebp),%edx

   10410:	8b 55 14             	mov    0x14(%ebp),%edx

   10419:	8b 55 18             	mov    0x18(%ebp),%edx
   1041c:	89 50 1c             	mov    %edx,0x1c(%eax)

    bitmap_init(&alloc->bitmap, bits, alloc->size / page_size, 0);

000104ae <addr_free_page>:

/**
 * @brief 释放多页内存
 */
static void addr_free_page(addr_alloc_t * alloc, uint32_t addr, int page_count)
{
   104ae:	55                   	push   %ebp
   104af:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   104b1:	53                   	push   %ebx
   104b2:	83 ec 14             	sub    $0x14,%esp
    mutex_lock(&alloc->mutex);
   104b5:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   104b8:	83 ec 0c             	sub    $0xc,%esp
   104bb:	50                   	push   %eax
   104bc:	e8 34 79 00 00       	call   17df5 <mutex_lock>
   104c1:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp

    // (addr- base_addr) / page_size = page_index, (待释放地址 - 基地址) / 页大小
    uint32_t page_index = (addr - alloc->start) / alloc->page_size;
   104c4:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   104c7:	8b 40 20             	mov    0x20(%eax),%eax
   104ca:	8b 55 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%edx
   104cd:	89 d1                	mov    %edx,%ecx
   104cf:	29 c1                	sub    %eax,%ecx
   104d1:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   104d4:	8b 58 1c             	mov    0x1c(%eax),%ebx
   104d7:	89 c8                	mov    %ecx,%eax
   104d9:	ba 00 00 00 00       	mov    $0x0,%edx
   104de:	f7 f3                	div    %ebx
   104e0:	89 45 f4             	mov    %eax,-0xc(%ebp)
    bitmap_set_bit(&alloc->bitmap, page_index, page_count, 0);
   104e3:	8b 45 f4             	mov    -0xc(%ebp),%eax
   104e6:	8b 55 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%edx
   104e9:	83 c2 14             	add    $0x14,%edx
   104ec:	6a 00                	push   $0x0
   104ee:	ff 75 10             	push   0x10(%ebp)
   104f1:	50                   	push   %eax
   104f2:	52                   	push   %edx
   104f3:	e8 0e 7c 00 00       	call   18106 <bitmap_set_bit>
   104f8:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp

    mutex_unlock(&alloc->mutex);
   104fb:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax

000105b2 <find_pte>:
 * pde_t * page_dir  当前进程页目录地址
 * uint32_t vaddr    虚拟地址
 * int alloc         是否要分配页表项, 1-分配, 0-不分配
 */
static pte_t * find_pte(pde_t * page_dir, uint32_t vaddr, int alloc)
{

/**
 * @brief 将任务加入睡眠状态
 */
void task_set_sleep(task_t * task, uint32_t ticks)
{
   11688:	55                   	push   %ebp
   11689:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   1168b:	83 ec 08             	sub    $0x8,%esp
    if (ticks <= 0)
   1168e:	83 7d 0c 00          	cmpl   $0x0,0xc(%ebp)
   11692:	74 2d                	je     116c1 <task_set_sleep+0x39>
     * 1) 将睡眠队列按照睡眠时间长短排序, 睡眠时长短的进程排在前面, 依次排好序后
     * 2) 第一个进程睡眠时间记为t1, 而第二个进程睡眠时间为t2+t1, 依次类推, 第三个进程睡眠时间为t3+t2+t1
     * 3) 当系统10ms心跳中断触发时只需将第一个进程睡眠时间t1-1, 相当于后面的进程睡眠时间也都-1
     * 4) 此时第一个进程若t1==0, 则换醒该进程, 将它放入就绪队列, 而第二个进程睡眠时间即为t2+t1(此时t1==0)
     */
    task->sleep_ticks = ticks;

   11697:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   1169a:	89 50 38             	mov    %edx,0x38(%eax)
    task->state = TASK_SLEEP;
   1169d:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   116a0:	c7 00 02 00 00 00    	movl   $0x2,(%eax)

000117f9 <task_dispatch>:
 * @brief 进行一次任务调度
 * 这里的调度算法采用最简单的方式: 按照进程管理的就绪队列中排队顺序来执行进程, 即每次都执行就绪队列中的第一个进程
 * 后续可通过不同的调度算法来优化进程切换性能
 */
void task_dispatch(void)
{

   117fa:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   117fc:	83 ec 18             	sub    $0x18,%esp
    //irq_state_t state = irq_enter_protection();

    task_t * to = task_next_run();
   117ff:	e8 3f fe ff ff       	call   11643 <task_next_run>
   11804:	89 45 f4             	mov    %eax,-0xc(%ebp)
    // to任务与当前任务不一样才用切换
    if (to != task_manager.curr_task)
   11807:	a1 00 d0 01 00       	mov    0x1d000,%eax
   1180c:	39 45 f4             	cmp    %eax,-0xc(%ebp)
   1180f:	74 2a                	je     1183b <task_dispatch+0x42>
    {
        // 组织好from, to任务
        task_t * from = task_current(); // from: 当前任务
   11811:	e8 d0 fe ff ff       	call   116e6 <task_current>
   11816:	89 45 f0             	mov    %eax,-0x10(%ebp)
        task_manager.curr_task = to; // to: 就绪队列第一个任务, 同时将当前任务设置为 to
   11819:	8b 45 f4             	mov    -0xc(%ebp),%eax
   1181c:	a3 00 d0 01 00       	mov    %eax,0x1d000

   11821:	8b 45 f4             	mov    -0xc(%ebp),%eax

000127ab <read_eflags>:

static inline uint32_t read_eflags(void)
{
   127ab:	55                   	push   %ebp
   127ac:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   127ae:	83 ec 10             	sub    $0x10,%esp
    uint32_t eflags;

    // CPU eflags寄存器值不可直接读取, 只能通过将该寄存器值先压入栈中, 再从栈中弹出到eax寄存器的方式获得
    __asm__ __volatile__("pushfl\n\tpopl %%eax":"=a"(eflags));
   127b1:	9c                   	pushf
   127b2:	58                   	pop    %eax
   127b3:	89 45 fc             	mov    %eax,-0x4(%ebp)
    return eflags;
   127b6:	8b 45 fc             	mov    -0x4(%ebp),%eax
}
   127b9:	c9                   	leave
   127ba:	c3                   	ret

000127bb <write_eflags>:

static inline void write_eflags(uint32_t eflags)
{
   127bb:	55                   	push   %ebp
   127bc:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
    // CPU eflags寄存器值不可直接写入, 需先将该值压入eax中, 然后再弹出给eflags寄存器
    __asm__ __volatile__("pushl %%eax\n\tpopfl"::"a"(eflags));
   127be:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   127c1:	50                   	push   %eax
   127c2:	9d                   	popf
}
   127c3:	90                   	nop
   127c4:	5d                   	pop    %ebp
   127c5:	c3                   	ret

000127c6 <dump_core_regs>:
 * 计算机中可能有很多设备, 每个设备都可能产生中断, 但是中断控制器的中断信号线是有限的
 * 这里没有实现中断控制器的 "多个设备共享一根中断信号线" 的功能
 */

static void dump_core_regs(exception_frame_t * frame)
{

   127c7:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   127c9:	57                   	push   %edi

   127cc:	83 ec 2c             	sub    $0x2c,%esp

    uint32_t esp, ss;
    if (frame->cs & 0x7) // 取得当前进程的代码段低3位的值>0, 说明特权级为3的进程(用户态进程)
   127cf:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   127d2:	8b 40 3c             	mov    0x3c(%eax),%eax
   127d5:	83 e0 07             	and    $0x7,%eax
   127d8:	85 c0                	test   %eax,%eax
   127da:	74 14                	je     127f0 <dump_core_regs+0x2a>
    {
        ss = frame->ds;
   127dc:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   127df:	8b 40 0c             	mov    0xc(%eax),%eax
   127e2:	89 45 e0             	mov    %eax,-0x20(%ebp)
        esp = frame->esp;

   127e8:	8b 40 1c             	mov    0x1c(%eax),%eax
   127eb:	89 45 e4             	mov    %eax,-0x1c(%ebp)
   127ee:	eb 12                	jmp    12802 <dump_core_regs+0x3c>
    }
    else
    {
        ss = frame->ss3;
   127f0:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   127f3:	8b 40 48             	mov    0x48(%eax),%eax
   127f6:	89 45 e0             	mov    %eax,-0x20(%ebp)
        esp = frame->esp3;
   127f9:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax

   127ff:	89 45 e4             	mov    %eax,-0x1c(%ebp)

   12823:	8b 30                	mov    (%eax),%esi

000128c5 <do_default_handler>:

static void do_default_handler(exception_frame_t * frame, const char * message)
{
   128c5:	55                   	push   %ebp
   128c6:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   128c8:	83 ec 08             	sub    $0x8,%esp
    log_printf("--------------------------------");
   128cb:	83 ec 0c             	sub    $0xc,%esp
   128ce:	68 b8 91 01 00       	push   $0x191b8
   128d3:	e8 d8 61 00 00       	call   18ab0 <log_printf>
   128d8:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp
    log_printf("IRQ/Exception happend: %s.", message);
   128db:	83 ec 08             	sub    $0x8,%esp
   128de:	ff 75 0c             	push   0xc(%ebp)
   128e1:	68 d9 91 01 00       	push   $0x191d9
   128e6:	e8 c5 61 00 00       	call   18ab0 <log_printf>
   128eb:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp
    dump_core_regs(frame);
   128ee:	83 ec 0c             	sub    $0xc,%esp
   128f1:	ff 75 08             	push   0x8(%ebp)
   128f4:	e8 cd fe ff ff       	call   127c6 <dump_core_regs>
   128f9:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp

    // todo: 留等以后补充打印任务栈的内容

    log_printf("--------------------------------");
   128fc:	83 ec 0c             	sub    $0xc,%esp
   128ff:	68 b8 91 01 00       	push   $0x191b8
   12904:	e8 a7 61 00 00       	call   18ab0 <log_printf>
   12909:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp
    if (frame->cs & 0x3)
   1290c:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   1290f:	8b 40 3c             	mov    0x3c(%eax),%eax
   12912:	83 e0 03             	and    $0x3,%eax
   12915:	85 c0                	test   %eax,%eax
   12917:	74 14                	je     1292d <do_default_handler+0x68>
    {
        sys_exit(frame->error_code);
   12919:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   1291c:	8b 40 34             	mov    0x34(%eax),%eax
   1291f:	83 ec 0c             	sub    $0xc,%esp
   12922:	50                   	push   %eax
   12923:	e8 31 fa ff ff       	call   12359 <sys_exit>
   12928:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp
        for (;;)
        {
            hlt(); // CPU进入停止运行状态, 该任务让出CPU, 减少当前任务的CPU占用率
        }
    }
}
   1292b:	eb 07                	jmp    12934 <do_default_handler+0x6f>
            hlt(); // CPU进入停止运行状态, 该任务让出CPU, 减少当前任务的CPU占用率

 */

/**
 * @brief 在IDT表中初始化对应中断号的中断/异常处理程序的中断描述符
 */
int irq_install(int irq_num, irq_handler_t handler)
{
   12f7a:	55                   	push   %ebp
   12f7b:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   12f7d:	83 ec 08             	sub    $0x8,%esp
    if (irq_num >= IDT_TABLE_NR)
   12f80:	81 7d 08 ff 00 00 00 	cmpl   $0xff,0x8(%ebp)
   12f87:	7e 07                	jle    12f90 <irq_install+0x16>
    {
        return -1;
   12f89:	b8 ff ff ff ff       	mov    $0xffffffff,%eax
   12f8e:	eb 25                	jmp    12fb5 <irq_install+0x3b>
     * GATE_P_PRESENT | GATE_DPL0 | GATE_TYPE_IDT - 中断属性
     */
    // IDTR: idt_table base + irq_num => idt descriptor(selector + offset)
    // selector(kernel_CS) => gdt_table +selector  => gdt descriptor(base)
    // base + offset
    gate_desc_set(idt_table + irq_num, KERNEL_SELECTOR_CS, (uint32_t) handler,
   12f90:	8b 45 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%eax
   12f93:	8b 55 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%edx
   12f96:	c1 e2 03             	shl    $0x3,%edx

   12f9f:	68 00 8e 00 00       	push   $0x8e00
   12fa4:	50                   	push   %eax

   12fa7:	52                   	push   %edx
   12fa8:	e8 c2 f5 ff ff       	call   1256f <gate_desc_set>
   12fad:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp
                  GATE_P_PRESENT | GATE_DPL0 | GATE_TYPE_IDT);
    return 0;
   12fb0:	b8 00 00 00 00       	mov    $0x0,%eax
}

000130df <irq_enter_protection>:

/**
 * @brief 进入中断保护, 读出当前CPU eflags寄存器状态信息, 然后再关中断
 */
irq_state_t irq_enter_protection(void)
{
   130df:	55                   	push   %ebp
   130e0:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   130e2:	83 ec 10             	sub    $0x10,%esp
    irq_state_t state = read_eflags(); // 读出CPU eflags寄存器状态信息
   130e5:	e8 c1 f6 ff ff       	call   127ab <read_eflags>
   130ea:	89 45 fc             	mov    %eax,-0x4(%ebp)
    // <<<Eflags Register>>>
    irq_disable_global(); // 关中断, 其实是修改了eflags寄存器的[9] bit位 - Interrupt Enable Flag
   130ed:	e8 d7 ff ff ff       	call   130c9 <irq_disable_global>
    return state;
   130f2:	8b 45 fc             	mov    -0x4(%ebp),%eax
}
   130f5:	c9                   	leave
   130f6:	c3                   	ret

   13102:	83 c4 04             	add    $0x4,%esp

00013a27 <write_esc>:

/**
 * 写入以ESC开头的序列
 */
static void write_esc(console_t * console, char c)
{
   13a27:	55                   	push   %ebp
   13a28:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   13a2a:	83 ec 18             	sub    $0x18,%esp
   13a2d:	8b 45 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%eax
   13a30:	88 45 f4             	mov    %al,-0xc(%ebp)
    // https://blog.csdn.net/ScilogyHunter/article/details/106874395
    // ESC状态处理, 转义序列模式 ESC 0x20-0x27(0或多个) 0x30-0x7e
    switch (c)
   13a33:	0f be 45 f4          	movsbl -0xc(%ebp),%eax
   13a37:	83 f8 5b             	cmp    $0x5b,%eax
   13a3a:	74 3f                	je     13a7b <write_esc+0x54>
   13a3c:	83 f8 5b             	cmp    $0x5b,%eax
   13a3f:	7f 54                	jg     13a95 <write_esc+0x6e>
   13a41:	83 f8 37             	cmp    $0x37,%eax
   13a44:	74 07                	je     13a4d <write_esc+0x26>
   13a46:	83 f8 38             	cmp    $0x38,%eax
   13a49:	74 19                	je     13a64 <write_esc+0x3d>
   13a4b:	eb 48                	jmp    13a95 <write_esc+0x6e>
    {
        case '7':        // ESC 7 保存光标
            save_cursor(console);
   13a4d:	ff 75 08             	push   0x8(%ebp)
   13a50:	e8 7f fd ff ff       	call   137d4 <save_cursor>
   13a55:	83 c4 04             	add    $0x4,%esp
            console->write_state = CONSOLE_WRITE_NORMAL;
   13a58:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   13a5b:	c7 40 04 00 00 00 00 	movl   $0x0,0x4(%eax)
            break;
   13a62:	eb 3c                	jmp    13aa0 <write_esc+0x79>
        case '8':        // ESC 8 恢复光标
            restore_cursor(console);
   13a64:	ff 75 08             	push   0x8(%ebp)
   13a67:	e8 86 fd ff ff       	call   137f2 <restore_cursor>
   13a6c:	83 c4 04             	add    $0x4,%esp
            console->write_state = CONSOLE_WRITE_NORMAL;
   13a6f:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   13a72:	c7 40 04 00 00 00 00 	movl   $0x0,0x4(%eax)
            break;
   13a79:	eb 25                	jmp    13aa0 <write_esc+0x79>
        case '[':
            clear_esc_param(console);
   13a7b:	83 ec 0c             	sub    $0xc,%esp
   13a7e:	ff 75 08             	push   0x8(%ebp)
   13a81:	e8 78 ff ff ff       	call   139fe <clear_esc_param>
   13a86:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp
            console->write_state = CONSOLE_WRITE_SQUARE;
   13a89:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   13a8c:	c7 40 04 02 00 00 00 	movl   $0x2,0x4(%eax)
            break;
   13a93:	eb 0b                	jmp    13aa0 <write_esc+0x79>
        default:
            console->write_state = CONSOLE_WRITE_NORMAL;
   13a95:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   13a98:	c7 40 04 00 00 00 00 	movl   $0x0,0x4(%eax)
            break;
   13a9f:	90                   	nop
    }
}

/**
 * @brief 等待磁盘有数据到达
 */
static inline int ata_wait_data(disk_t * disk)
{

   1439c:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   1439e:	83 ec 10             	sub    $0x10,%esp
    uint8_t status;

    do
    {
        // 等待数据或者有错误
        status = inb(DISK_STATUS(disk));

   143a4:	0f b7 40 24          	movzwl 0x24(%eax),%eax

        if ((status & (DISK_STATUS_BUSY | DISK_STATUS_DRQ | DISK_STATUS_ERR)) != DISK_STATUS_BUSY)

   143c3:	3d 80 00 00 00       	cmp    $0x80,%eax
   143c8:	75 02                	jne    143cc <ata_wait_data+0x31>

        {
            break;
   143cc:	90                   	nop
        }

00015131 <pit_init>:
static uint32_t sys_tick;    // 系统启动后的tick数量



static void pit_init(void)
{
   15131:	55                   	push   %ebp
   15132:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   15134:	83 ec 10             	sub    $0x10,%esp
    uint32_t reload_count = PIT_OSC_FREQ / (1000.0 / OS_TICK_MS); // 此处设置每隔10ms产生一次中断
   15137:	c7 45 fc 9b 2e 00 00 	movl   $0x2e9b,-0x4(%ebp)

    // 按照8253定时器芯片手册中的说明初始化定时器硬件参数寄存器, 使用模式3
    // 定时器芯片一般有3个定时器, 这里只使用到定时器0 Timer_0
    outb(PIT_COMMAND_MODE_PORT, PIT_CHANNLE0 | PIT_LOAD_LOHI | PIT_MODE3);
   1513e:	6a 36                	push   $0x36
   15140:	6a 43                	push   $0x43
   15142:	e8 cb ff ff ff       	call   15112 <outb>
   15147:	83 c4 08             	add    $0x8,%esp
     * 定时器芯片工作原理
     * 1) 使用reload_count初始化芯片中的计数器寄存器, 随着时间的推移 定时器芯片中的计数器不断减少
     * 2) 当 计数器 减少到0时就会触发中断通知中断控制器
     * 3) 同时定时器芯片中的硬件模块会重新加载reload_count到计数器寄存器, 这样就形成周期性中断的定时器
     */
    outb(PIT_CHANNEL0_DATA_PORT, reload_count & 0xFF);        // 先加载低8位
   1514a:	8b 45 fc             	mov    -0x4(%ebp),%eax
   1514d:	0f b6 c0             	movzbl %al,%eax
   15150:	50                   	push   %eax
   15151:	6a 40                	push   $0x40
   15153:	e8 ba ff ff ff       	call   15112 <outb>
   15158:	83 c4 08             	add    $0x8,%esp
    outb(PIT_CHANNEL0_DATA_PORT, (reload_count >> 8) & 0xFF); // 再加载高8位
   1515b:	8b 45 fc             	mov    -0x4(%ebp),%eax
   1515e:	c1 e8 08             	shr    $0x8,%eax
   15161:	0f b6 c0             	movzbl %al,%eax
   15164:	50                   	push   %eax
   15165:	6a 40                	push   $0x40
   15167:	e8 a6 ff ff ff       	call   15112 <outb>
   1516c:	83 c4 08             	add    $0x8,%esp
}
   1516f:	90                   	nop
   15170:	c9                   	leave
   15171:	c3                   	ret

    sys_tick++;

/**
 * @brief 转换文件名为diritem中的短文件名，如a.txt 转换成a      txt
 */
static void to_sfn(char* dest, const char* src) {
   15de1:	55                   	push   %ebp
   15de2:	89 e5                	mov    %esp,%ebp
   15de4:	83 ec 18             	sub    $0x18,%esp

   15dea:	6a 0b                	push   $0xb

   15df1:	e8 0b 27 00 00       	call   18501 <kernel_memset>
   15df6:	83 c4 10             	add    $0x10,%esp

    // 不断生成直到遇到分隔符和写完缓存
    char * curr = dest;
   15df9:	8b 45 08             	mov    0x8(%ebp),%eax
   15dfc:	89 45 f4             	mov    %eax,-0xc(%ebp)
    char * end = dest + SFN_LEN;

   15e02:	83 c0 0b             	add    $0xb,%eax
   15e05:	89 45 ec             	mov    %eax,-0x14(%ebp)
    while (*src && (curr < end)) {
   15e08:	eb 49                	jmp    15e53 <to_sfn+0x72>
        char c = *src++;
   15e0a:	8b 45 0c             	mov    0xc(%ebp),%eax
   15e0d:	8d 50 01             	lea    0x1(%eax),%edx
   15e10:	89 55 0c             	mov    %edx,0xc(%ebp)
   15e13:	0f b6 00             	movzbl (%eax),%eax
   15e16:	88 45 f3             	mov    %al,-0xd(%ebp)

        switch (c) {
   15e19:	0f be 45 f3          	movsbl -0xd(%ebp),%eax
   15e1d:	83 f8 2e             	cmp    $0x2e,%eax
   15e20:	75 0b                	jne    15e2d <to_sfn+0x4c>
        case '.':       // 隔附，跳到扩展名区，不写字符
            curr = dest + 8;

int kernel_strncmp(const char * s1, const char * s2, int size)
